"""
backend/api.py
REST API endpoints consumed by the SOC dashboard frontend.
All endpoints require a valid JWT (any role) unless noted.
"""
import csv
import io
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import psutil
from flask import Blueprint, jsonify, request, make_response
from flask_jwt_extended import (
    get_jwt,
    get_jwt_identity,
    verify_jwt_in_request
)
from flask_limiter import Limiter
from flask_limiter.util import get_remote_address
from db.models import db, Alert, BlockedIP, WebAccessLog, AuditLog
from feature_engine.aggregator import aggregator
from backend.auth import roles_required
logger = logging.getLogger("ai_idps.api")
api_bp = Blueprint("api", __name__)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Rate limiter (attached to app in create_app via limiter.init_app)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# MITRE ATT&CK mapping
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
MITRE_MAP = {
    "PortScan":          "T1046 - Network Service Discovery",
    "DoS":               "T1499 - Endpoint Denial of Service",
    "BruteForce":        "T1110 - Brute Force",
    "WebAttack":         "T1190 - Exploit Public-Facing Application",
    "SQL_INJECTION":     "T1190 - Exploit Public-Facing Application",
    "XSS":               "T1189 - Drive-by Compromise",
    "XSS_REFLECTED":     "T1189 - Drive-by Compromise",
    "XSS_STORED":        "T1189 - Drive-by Compromise",
    "COMMAND_INJECTION": "T1059 - Command and Scripting Interpreter",
    "LFI":               "T1005 - Data from Local System",
    "Normal":            None,
}
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Helper
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _write_audit(action, target=None, detail=None):
    try:
        verify_jwt_in_request(optional=True)
        try:
            claims = get_jwt()
            username = get_jwt_identity() or "anonymous"
            role = claims.get("role", "anonymous")
        except Exception:
            username = "anonymous"
            role = "anonymous"
        entry = AuditLog(
            username=username,
            role=role,
            action=action,
            target=target,
            detail=json.dumps(detail) if detail else None,
            ip_address=request.remote_addr,
        )
        db.session.add(entry)
        db.session.commit()
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning("Audit log write failed: %s", e)

@api_bp.route("/api/system/stats", methods=["GET"])
@limiter.limit("60 per minute")
def system_stats():
    try:
        cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.2)
        mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
        net = psutil.net_io_counters()
        return jsonify({
            "cpu_percent":  cpu,
            "ram_percent":  mem.percent,
            "ram_used_mb":  round(mem.used  / (1024 * 1024), 2),
            "ram_total_mb": round(mem.total / (1024 * 1024), 2),
            "bytes_sent":   net.bytes_sent,
            "bytes_recv":   net.bytes_recv,
        })
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("system_stats error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/traffic/live", methods=["GET"])
@limiter.limit("30 per minute")
def live_traffic():
    """Aggregate live feature snapshot for every actively-seen IP."""
    try:
        result = []
        for ip in aggregator.active_ips():
            feats = aggregator.get_features(ip)
            feats["ip"] = ip
            result.append(feats)
        return jsonify(result)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("live_traffic error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/stats/summary", methods=["GET"])
@limiter.limit("30 per minute")
def stats_summary():
    """KPI summary for Page 1 header cards."""
    try:
        since = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(hours=24)
        active_alerts  = Alert.query.filter(Alert.timestamp >= since).count()
        blocked_count  = BlockedIP.query.filter_by(active=True).count()
        critical_count = Alert.query.filter(
            Alert.timestamp >= since, Alert.severity == "critical"
        ).count()
        # Top attacking IP in last 24h
        top_ip_row = (
            db.session.query(Alert.src_ip, db.func.count(Alert.id).label("cnt"))
            .filter(Alert.timestamp >= since)
            .group_by(Alert.src_ip)
            .order_by(db.desc("cnt"))
            .first()
        )
        top_ip = top_ip_row[0] if top_ip_row else "N/A"
        top_ip_count = top_ip_row[1] if top_ip_row else 0
        return jsonify({
            "active_alerts_24h": active_alerts,
            "blocked_ips":       blocked_count,
            "critical_alerts":   critical_count,
            "top_attacker_ip":   top_ip,
            "top_attacker_hits": top_ip_count,
        })
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("stats_summary error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
# ===========================================================================
# Page 2 â€“ Attack analytics
# ===========================================================================
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Dashboard time-window helper
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# All dashboard stat endpoints default to showing only the last 24 hours of
# data so older alerts don't linger on the SOC Overview / Analytics pages.
# Callers can override with ?hours=N or an explicit ?since=ISO8601.
DEFAULT_DASHBOARD_WINDOW_HOURS = 24


def _dashboard_since(default_hours=DEFAULT_DASHBOARD_WINDOW_HOURS):
    """Resolve the cutoff datetime for dashboard queries.
    ?since=<ISO8601> takes priority if provided and valid.
    Otherwise falls back to now - ?hours=N (default 24)."""
    since_str = request.args.get("since")
    if since_str:
        try:
            return datetime.fromisoformat(since_str)
        except ValueError:
            pass
    try:
        hours = float(request.args.get("hours", default_hours))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        hours = default_hours
    return datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(hours=hours)


@api_bp.route("/api/alerts", methods=["GET"])
@limiter.limit("60 per minute")
def list_alerts():
    try:
        page       = max(1, int(request.args.get("page", 1)))
        per_page   = min(200, int(request.args.get("per_page", 50)))
        attack_type = request.args.get("attack_type")
        severity    = request.args.get("severity")
        since       = _dashboard_since()
        q = Alert.query.order_by(Alert.timestamp.desc()).filter(Alert.timestamp >= since)
        if attack_type:
            q = q.filter(Alert.attack_type == attack_type)
        if severity:
            q = q.filter(Alert.severity == severity)
        paginated = q.paginate(page=page, per_page=per_page, error_out=False)
        return jsonify({
            "page":       paginated.page,
            "per_page":   paginated.per_page,
            "total":      paginated.total,
            "pages":      paginated.pages,
            "alerts": [
                {
                    "id":             a.id,
                    "timestamp":      a.timestamp.isoformat(),
                    "src_ip":         a.src_ip,
                    "attack_type":    a.attack_type,
                    "confidence":     round(a.confidence, 3),
                    "severity":       a.severity,
                    "action_taken":   a.action_taken,
                    "mitre_technique": MITRE_MAP.get(a.attack_type),
                }
                for a in paginated.items
            ],
        })
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("list_alerts error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/alerts/distribution", methods=["GET"])
def alert_distribution():
    try:
        since = _dashboard_since()
        rows = (
            db.session.query(Alert.attack_type, db.func.count(Alert.id))
            .filter(Alert.timestamp >= since)
            .group_by(Alert.attack_type)
            .all()
        )
        return jsonify({at: cnt for at, cnt in rows})
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("alert_distribution error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/alerts/severity", methods=["GET"])
def alert_severity():
    """Count of alerts grouped by severity."""
    try:
        since = _dashboard_since()
        rows = (
            db.session.query(Alert.severity, db.func.count(Alert.id))
            .filter(Alert.timestamp >= since)
            .group_by(Alert.severity)
            .all()
        )
        return jsonify({sev: cnt for sev, cnt in rows})
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("alert_severity error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/alerts/timeline", methods=["GET"])
def alert_timeline():
    """Alerts grouped by hour, for Page 2 trend chart."""
    try:
        since = _dashboard_since()
        rows = (
            db.session.query(
                db.func.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:00:00", Alert.timestamp).label("bucket"),
                Alert.attack_type,
                db.func.count(Alert.id),
            )
            .filter(Alert.timestamp >= since)
            .group_by("bucket", Alert.attack_type)
            .order_by("bucket")
            .all()
        )
        timeline = {}
        for bucket, attack_type, count in rows:
            key = str(bucket)
            timeline.setdefault(key, {})[attack_type] = count
        return jsonify(timeline)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("alert_timeline error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/alerts/top-sources", methods=["GET"])
def top_sources():
    """Top attacking source IPs by alert count."""
    try:
        since = _dashboard_since()
        limit = min(20, int(request.args.get("limit", 10)))
        rows = (
            db.session.query(Alert.src_ip, db.func.count(Alert.id).label("cnt"))
            .filter(Alert.timestamp >= since)
            .group_by(Alert.src_ip)
            .order_by(db.desc("cnt"))
            .limit(limit)
            .all()
        )
        return jsonify([{"ip": ip, "count": cnt} for ip, cnt in rows])
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("top_sources error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/alerts/blocked-vs-allowed", methods=["GET"])
def blocked_vs_allowed():
    """Counts of alerts by action_taken (blocked vs alerted vs none)."""
    try:
        since = _dashboard_since()
        rows = (
            db.session.query(Alert.action_taken, db.func.count(Alert.id))
            .filter(Alert.timestamp >= since)
            .group_by(Alert.action_taken)
            .all()
        )
        return jsonify({action: cnt for action, cnt in rows})
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("blocked_vs_allowed error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/alerts/heatmap", methods=["GET"])
def alerts_heatmap():
    """Alerts by hour-of-day (0-23) and attack type for the heatmap."""
    try:
        since = _dashboard_since()
        rows = (
            db.session.query(
                db.func.strftime("%H", Alert.timestamp).label("hour"),
                Alert.attack_type,
                db.func.count(Alert.id).label("cnt"),
            )
            .filter(Alert.timestamp >= since)
            .group_by("hour", Alert.attack_type)
            .all()
        )
        result = {}
        for hour, attack_type, cnt in rows:
            h = int(hour)
            result.setdefault(attack_type, {})[h] = cnt
        return jsonify(result)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("alerts_heatmap error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/alerts/confidence-distribution", methods=["GET"])
def confidence_distribution():
    """Histogram of confidence scores in 10% buckets."""
    try:
        since = _dashboard_since()
        alerts = Alert.query.filter(Alert.timestamp >= since).with_entities(Alert.confidence).all()
        buckets = {f"{i*10}-{i*10+10}%": 0 for i in range(10)}
        for (conf,) in alerts:
            if conf is not None:
                idx = min(int(conf * 10), 9)
                key = f"{idx*10}-{idx*10+10}%"
                buckets[key] += 1
        return jsonify(buckets)
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("confidence_distribution error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/alerts/export/csv", methods=["GET"])
@limiter.limit("10 per minute")
def export_alerts_csv():
    """Download all alerts as CSV."""
    try:
        _write_audit("EXPORT_CSV", target="alerts")
        alerts = Alert.query.order_by(Alert.timestamp.desc()).limit(10000).all()
        output = io.StringIO()
        writer = csv.writer(output)
        writer.writerow(["id", "timestamp", "src_ip", "dst_ip", "attack_type",
                          "confidence", "severity", "action_taken", "mitre_technique"])
        for a in alerts:
            writer.writerow([
                a.id, a.timestamp.isoformat(), a.src_ip, a.dst_ip,
                a.attack_type, round(a.confidence, 4) if a.confidence else "",
                a.severity, a.action_taken,
                MITRE_MAP.get(a.attack_type, ""),
            ])
        resp = make_response(output.getvalue())
        resp.headers["Content-Type"] = "text/csv"
        resp.headers["Content-Disposition"] = "attachment; filename=alerts_export.csv"
        return resp
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("export_alerts_csv error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500

# Page 3 â€“ Incident forensics
# ===========================================================================
@api_bp.route("/api/forensics/<ip>", methods=["GET"])
@limiter.limit("30 per minute")
def ip_forensics(ip):
    """Full timeline of alerts + traffic + web requests for a given IP."""
    try:
        alerts = (
            Alert.query.filter_by(src_ip=ip)
            .order_by(Alert.timestamp.desc())
            .limit(200)
            .all()
        )
        web_logs = (
            WebAccessLog.query.filter_by(src_ip=ip)
            .order_by(WebAccessLog.timestamp.desc())
            .limit(200)
            .all()
        )
        # Compute risk score (0â€“100)
        severity_weights = {"critical": 40, "high": 20, "medium": 10, "low": 3}
        raw_score = sum(severity_weights.get(a.severity, 0) for a in alerts)
        if any(a.action_taken == "blocked" for a in alerts):
            raw_score += 20
        risk_score = min(100, raw_score)
        # Unique MITRE techniques
        mitre_techniques = list({
            MITRE_MAP[a.attack_type]
            for a in alerts
            if a.attack_type in MITRE_MAP and MITRE_MAP[a.attack_type]
        })
        return jsonify({
            "ip":    ip,
            "risk_score":       risk_score,
            "mitre_techniques": mitre_techniques,
            "alert_count":      len(alerts),
            "web_request_count": len(web_logs),
            "alerts": [
                {
                    "timestamp":       a.timestamp.isoformat(),
                    "attack_type":     a.attack_type,
                    "confidence":      round(a.confidence, 3),
                    "severity":        a.severity,
                    "features":        json.loads(a.raw_features) if a.raw_features else {},
                    "action_taken":    a.action_taken,
                    "mitre_technique": MITRE_MAP.get(a.attack_type),
                }
                for a in alerts
            ],
            "web_requests": [
                {
                    "timestamp":   w.timestamp.isoformat(),
                    "method":      w.method,
                    "path":        w.path,
                    "status_code": w.status_code,
                    "user_agent":  w.user_agent,
                }
                for w in web_logs
            ],
        })
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("ip_forensics error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/forensics/<ip>/incident-report", methods=["GET"])
@limiter.limit("10 per minute")
def incident_report_pdf(ip):
    """Generate and download a PDF incident report for an IP."""
    try:
        from fpdf import FPDF
        alerts = (
            Alert.query.filter_by(src_ip=ip)
            .order_by(Alert.timestamp.desc())
            .limit(100)
            .all()
        )
        _write_audit("EXPORT_INCIDENT_PDF", target=ip)
        pdf = FPDF()
        pdf.add_page()
        pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 16)
        pdf.cell(0, 10, "AI-IDPS â€” Incident Report", ln=True)
        pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 10)
        pdf.cell(0, 7, f"Generated: {datetime.utcnow().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')} UTC", ln=True)
        pdf.cell(0, 7, f"Target IP: {ip}", ln=True)
        pdf.cell(0, 7, f"Total Alerts: {len(alerts)}", ln=True)
        pdf.ln(4)
        severity_weights = {"critical": 40, "high": 20, "medium": 10, "low": 3}
        risk_score = min(100, sum(severity_weights.get(a.severity, 0) for a in alerts))
        pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 12)
        pdf.cell(0, 8, f"Risk Score: {risk_score}/100", ln=True)
        pdf.ln(3)
        pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "B", 11)
        pdf.cell(0, 7, "Alert Timeline", ln=True)
        pdf.set_font("Helvetica", "", 9)
        pdf.set_fill_color(30, 30, 50)
        for a in alerts[:50]:
            line = (f"{a.timestamp.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}  "
                    f"{a.attack_type:<14} {a.severity:<10} "
                    f"conf={a.confidence:.2f}  action={a.action_taken}")
            pdf.cell(0, 6, line, ln=True)
        pdf_bytes = pdf.output()
        resp = make_response(bytes(pdf_bytes))
        resp.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/pdf"
        resp.headers["Content-Disposition"] = f"attachment; filename=incident_{ip}.pdf"
        return resp
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("incident_report_pdf error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500

@api_bp.route("/api/blocked-ips", methods=["GET"])
def blocked_ips():
    try:
        rows = BlockedIP.query.order_by(BlockedIP.blocked_at.desc()).all()
        return jsonify([
            {
                "ip_address": b.ip_address,
                "reason":     b.reason,
                "blocked_at": b.blocked_at.isoformat(),
                "expires_at": b.expires_at.isoformat() if b.expires_at else None,
                "active":     b.active,
            }
            for b in rows
        ])
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("blocked_ips error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/blocked-ips/<ip>/unblock", methods=["POST"])
@roles_required("admin", "analyst")
def unblock_ip(ip):
    try:
        from flask import current_app
        ips_engine = current_app.config["IPS_ENGINE"]
        ok = ips_engine.unblock_ip(ip)
        if not ok:
            return jsonify({"error": "IP not currently blocked"}), 404
        _write_audit("UNBLOCK_IP", target=ip)
        return jsonify({"message": f"{ip} unblocked"})
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("unblock_ip error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
@api_bp.route("/api/blocked-ips/<ip>/block", methods=["POST"])
@roles_required("admin", "analyst")
def manual_block_ip(ip):
    try:
        from flask import current_app
        ips_engine = current_app.config["IPS_ENGINE"]
        existing   = BlockedIP.query.filter_by(ip_address=ip, active=True).first()
        if existing:
            return jsonify({"error": "already blocked"}), 409
        data    = request.get_json() or {}
        reason  = data.get("reason", "manual block")
        timeout = data.get("timeout_minutes")   # optional timeout
        rule_id    = None
        expires_at = None
        if timeout:
            try:
                expires_at = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=int(timeout))
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                return jsonify({"error": "timeout_minutes must be an integer"}), 400
        if not ips_engine.dry_run:
            try:
                rule_id = ips_engine._add_to_opnsense_alias(ip)
            except Exception as e:
                return jsonify({"error": f"OPNsense error: {e}"}), 502
        blocked = BlockedIP(
            ip_address       = ip,
            reason           = reason,
            blocked_at       = datetime.utcnow(),
            expires_at       = expires_at,
            opnsense_rule_id = rule_id,
            active           = True,
        )
        db.session.add(blocked)
        db.session.commit()
        _write_audit("BLOCK_IP", target=ip, detail={"reason": reason, "timeout_minutes": timeout})
        return jsonify({"message": f"{ip} blocked", "expires_at": expires_at.isoformat() if expires_at else None})
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("manual_block_ip error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500
# ===========================================================================

@api_bp.route("/api/audit-log", methods=["GET"])
@roles_required("admin")
def audit_log():
    try:
        page     = max(1, int(request.args.get("page", 1)))
        per_page = min(100, int(request.args.get("per_page", 50)))
        paginated = (
            AuditLog.query
            .order_by(AuditLog.timestamp.desc())
            .paginate(page=page, per_page=per_page, error_out=False)
        )
        return jsonify({
            "page":     paginated.page,
            "total":    paginated.total,
            "entries": [
                {
                    "timestamp":  e.timestamp.isoformat(),
                    "username":   e.username,
                    "role":       e.role,
                    "action":     e.action,
                    "target":     e.target,
                    "ip_address": e.ip_address,
                }
                for e in paginated.items
            ],
        })
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error("audit_log error: %s", e)
        return jsonify({"error": "Internal server error"}), 500

"""
backend/app.py
"""
import os
import sys
import logging
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s",
)
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from flask import Flask, render_template, redirect, url_for
from flask_socketio import SocketIO
from flask_jwt_extended import JWTManager
from flask_cors import CORS
from backend.config import Config
from db.models import db, Alert, BlockedIP
from backend.auth import auth_bp, create_default_admin
from backend.api import api_bp, limiter
from backend.test_site import test_site_bp
from capture.sniffer import start_sniffer, packet_queue
from feature_engine.aggregator import start_packet_consumer
from ids.engine import IDSEngine
from backend.log_monitor import ApacheLogMonitor
from ips.engine import IPSEngine
def create_app():
    app = Flask(
        __name__,
        template_folder=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "dashboard", "templates"),
        static_folder=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "dashboard", "static"),
    )
    app.config.from_object(Config)
    db.init_app(app)
    JWTManager(app)
    CORS(app, origins=app.config.get("CORS_ORIGINS", "*"), supports_credentials=True)
    limiter.init_app(app)
    app.register_blueprint(auth_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(api_bp)
    app.register_blueprint(test_site_bp)
    socketio = SocketIO(app, cors_allowed_origins="*", async_mode="eventlet")
    @app.route("/")
    def index():
        return redirect(url_for("login_page"))
    @app.route("/login")
    def login_page():
        return render_template("login.html")
    @app.route("/dashboard")
    def dashboard_page1():
        return render_template("page1_live.html")
    @app.route("/dashboard/analytics")
    def dashboard_page2():
        return render_template("page2_analytics.html")
    @app.route("/dashboard/forensics")
    def dashboard_page3():
        return render_template("page3_forensics.html")
    @socketio.on("connect", namespace="/live")
    def handle_connect():
        logging.getLogger("ai_idps.ws").info("WebSocket client connected")
    @socketio.on("disconnect", namespace="/live")
    def handle_disconnect():
        logging.getLogger("ai_idps.ws").info("WebSocket client disconnected")
    return app, socketio
def bootstrap_engines(app, socketio):
    with app.app_context():
        db.create_all()
        create_default_admin()
        ips_engine = IPSEngine(
            db, BlockedIP, socketio,
            dry_run=app.config["IPS_DRY_RUN"],
            block_thresholds=app.config.get("IPS_BLOCK_THRESHOLDS"),
        )
        app.config["IPS_ENGINE"] = ips_engine
        ids_engine = IDSEngine(app, db, Alert, socketio, ips_engine=ips_engine)
        start_sniffer(iface=app.config.get("CAPTURE_IFACE"))
        start_packet_consumer(packet_queue)
        ids_engine.start()
        log_monitor = ApacheLogMonitor(app.config["APACHE_ACCESS_LOG"])
        log_monitor.start()
        logging.getLogger("ai_idps").info(
        "IDPS engines started. IPS dry-run=%s",
        ips_engine.dry_run,
)
def main():
    app, socketio = create_app()
    bootstrap_engines(app, socketio)
    socketio.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=5000, debug=False)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
backend/auth.py

JWT-based authentication and role-based access control.
Roles: admin, analyst, viewer
"""

from functools import wraps
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify
from flask_jwt_extended import (
    create_access_token,
    jwt_required,
    get_jwt,
)

from db.models import db, User

auth_bp = Blueprint("auth", __name__)


@auth_bp.route("/api/auth/register", methods=["POST"])
@jwt_required()
def register():
    """Only an admin can create new users."""
    claims = get_jwt()
    if claims.get("role") != "admin":
        return jsonify({"error": "Admin role required"}), 403

    data = request.get_json() or {}
    username = data.get("username")
    password = data.get("password")
    role = data.get("role", "viewer")

    if not username or not password:
        return jsonify({"error": "username and password required"}), 400

    if role not in ("admin", "analyst", "viewer"):
        return jsonify({"error": "invalid role"}), 400

    if User.query.filter_by(username=username).first():
        return jsonify({"error": "user already exists"}), 409

    user = User(username=username, role=role)
    user.set_password(password)
    db.session.add(user)
    db.session.commit()

    return jsonify({"message": "user created", "username": username, "role": role}), 201


@auth_bp.route("/api/auth/login", methods=["POST"])
def login():
    data = request.get_json() or {}
    username = data.get("username")
    password = data.get("password")

    user = User.query.filter_by(username=username).first()
    if not user or not user.check_password(password):
        return jsonify({"error": "invalid credentials"}), 401

    token = create_access_token(
        identity=user.username,
        additional_claims={"role": user.role, "user_id": user.id},
    )

    return jsonify({"access_token": token, "role": user.role, "username": user.username})


def roles_required(*allowed_roles):
    """Decorator: restrict an endpoint to specific roles."""

    def wrapper(fn):
        @wraps(fn)
        @jwt_required()
        def decorated(*args, **kwargs):
            claims = get_jwt()
            if claims.get("role") not in allowed_roles:
                return jsonify({"error": "insufficient permissions"}), 403
            return fn(*args, **kwargs)

        return decorated

    return wrapper


def create_default_admin():
    """Create a default admin user if none exists (first run)."""
    if not User.query.filter_by(role="admin").first():
        admin = User(username="admin", role="admin")
        admin.set_password("ChangeMe123!")
        db.session.add(admin)
        db.session.commit()
       # print("[Auth] Created default admin user -> username: admin / password: ChangeMe123!")
        #print("[Auth] CHANGE THIS PASSWORD IMMEDIATELY.")

def _warn_insecure():

    jwt_sec = os.environ.get("JWT_SECRET_KEY", _DEFAULT_JWT)
    issues  = []
    if secret == _DEFAULT_SECRET:
        issues.append("SECRET_KEY is using the insecure default value")
    if jwt_sec == _DEFAULT_JWT:
        issues.append("JWT_SECRET_KEY is using the insecure default value")
    if issues:
        print("\n" + "=" * 60, file=sys.stderr)
        print("  âš   SECURITY WARNING â€” AI-IDPS", file=sys.stderr)
        for i in issues:
            print(f"  â€¢  {i}", file=sys.stderr)
        print("  Set SECRET_KEY and JWT_SECRET_KEY in your .env file", file=sys.stderr)
        print("=" * 60 + "\n", file=sys.stderr)
_warn_insecure()

"""
backend/log_monitor.py
Tails the Apache access.log and feeds parsed web requests into the
feature aggregator for rule-based + ML detection.
"""
import os
import time
import threading
import logging
import re
from urllib.parse import unquote
from feature_engine.aggregator import aggregator

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

LOG_PATH_DEFAULT = r"C:\xampp\apache\logs\access.log"

COMBINED_LOG_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r'(?P<ip>\S+)\s+'
    r'\S+\s+'
    r'\S+\s+'
    r'\[(?P<timestamp>[^\]]+)\]\s+'
    r'"(?P<method>\S+)\s+(?P<path>\S+)\s+\S+"\s+'
    r'(?P<status>\d{3})\s+'
    r'(?P<size>\S+)'
    r'(?:\s+"(?P<referer>[^"]*)"\s+"(?P<useragent>[^"]*)")?'
)

SQL_INJECTION_PATTERNS = [
    "union",
    "select",
    "sleep(",
    "benchmark(",
    "or 1=1",
    "--",
]
XSS_PATTERNS = [
    "<script",
    "onerror=",
    "javascript:",
]
TRAVERSAL_PATTERNS = [
    "../",
    "..\\",
]
ADMIN_PATHS = [
    "/admin",
    "/wp-admin",
    "/phpmyadmin",
]


class ApacheLogMonitor:
    def __init__(self, log_path=LOG_PATH_DEFAULT):
        self.log_path = log_path
        self._running = False
        self._thread = None
        logger.info("Loaded Apache log monitor")

    def start(self):
        self._running = True
        self._thread = threading.Thread(
            target=self._monitor,
            daemon=True,
            name="ApacheLogMonitor"
        )
        self._thread.start()
        logger.info("Watching: %s", self.log_path)

    def stop(self):
        self._running = False
        if self._thread is not None:
            self._thread.join(timeout=5)
            self._thread = None

    def _monitor(self):
        file_handle = None
        try:
            while self._running:
                try:
                    if not os.path.exists(self.log_path):
                        if file_handle is not None:
                            file_handle.close()
                            file_handle = None
                        time.sleep(2)
                        continue
                    if file_handle is None:
                        file_handle = open(self.log_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
                        file_handle.seek(0, 2)
                    line = file_handle.readline()
                    if not line:
                        time.sleep(0.5)
                        continue
                    line = line.rstrip("\n").rstrip("\r")
                    if not line:
                        continue
                    parsed = self._parse_line(line)
                    if parsed:
                        self._process_request(parsed)
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.error("Error in monitor loop: %s", exc)
                    if file_handle is not None:
                        try:
                            file_handle.close()
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                        file_handle = None
                    time.sleep(2)
        finally:
            if file_handle is not None:
                try:
                    file_handle.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def _parse_line(self, line):
        match = COMBINED_LOG_PATTERN.match(line)
        if not match:
            return None
        try:
            size_raw = match.group("size")
            size = int(size_raw) if size_raw != "-" else 0
            return {
                "ip": match.group("ip"),
                "timestamp": match.group("timestamp"),
                "method": match.group("method"),
                "path": match.group("path"),
                "raw": line,
                "status": int(match.group("status")),
                "size": size,
                "useragent": match.group("useragent") or "",
            }
        except Exception:
            return None

    def _calculate_score(self, path, status):
        # CRITICAL: browsers/Apache log paths URL-encoded
        # (e.g. "<script>" becomes "%3Cscript%3E"). Without decoding here,
        # every pattern check below silently never matches real attack
        # traffic - this was why DVWA attacks weren't being detected at
        # all even though the log lines were being read correctly.
        path_decoded = unquote(path)
        score = 0
        path_lower = path_decoded.lower()
        for pattern in SQL_INJECTION_PATTERNS:
            if pattern in path_lower:
                score += 20
                break
        for pattern in XSS_PATTERNS:
            if pattern in path_lower:
                score += 20
                break
        for pattern in TRAVERSAL_PATTERNS:
            if pattern in path_lower:
                score += 20
                break
        for admin_path in ADMIN_PATHS:
            if path_lower.startswith(admin_path):
                score += 5
                break
        if status == 401:
            score += 10
        elif status == 403:
            score += 20
        elif status == 500:
            score += 25
        return min(score, 100)

    def _process_request(self, parsed):
        ip = parsed["ip"]
        status = parsed["status"]
        path = parsed["path"]
        method = parsed["method"]
        suspicious_score = self._calculate_score(path, status)
        if suspicious_score >= 50:
            logger.warning(
                "[WEB ATTACK] %s %s %s score=%d",
                ip, method, path, suspicious_score
            )
        try:
            # Pass the URL-decoded path/query into the aggregator so its
            # own sql/xss/cmd/lfi pattern matching (which also looks for
            # plain-text patterns like "<script" or "union") can actually
            # see the real payload instead of its percent-encoded form.
            decoded_path = unquote(path)
            aggregator.add_web_request(
                ip=ip,
                status_code=status,
                path=decoded_path,
                suspicious_score=suspicious_score,
                method=method,
                user_agent=parsed.get("useragent", ""),
                query="",  # Apache's logged request target already includes
                           # the query string as part of `path` - passing it
                           # again here would double-count keyword matches.
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Failed to add web request to aggregator: %s", exc)
        if status == 401:
            try:
                aggregator.add_failed_login(ip)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error("Failed to add failed login to aggregator: %s", exc)

from flask import Blueprint, request

test_site_bp = Blueprint("test_site", __name__)
FAKE_USERS = {"admin": "password123", "user": "letmein"}
ADMIN_PATHS = ["/admin", "/administrator", "/wp-admin", "/phpmyadmin"]

def log_request(status_code):
    src_ip = request.headers.get("X-Forwarded-For", request.remote_addr)
    path = request.path
    method = request.method
    target = build_target_string(path, request.query_string.decode("utf-8","ignore"), request.get_data(as_text=True), request.headers)
    score, matched = score_request(target)
    try:
        log = WebAccessLog(src_ip=src_ip, method=method, path=request.full_path, status_code=status_code, user_agent=request.headers.get("User-Agent",""), body_snippet=(request.get_data(as_text=True) or "")[:500])
        db.session.add(log)
        db.session.commit()
    except Exception:
        db.session.rollback()
    body_text = request.get_data(as_text=True) or ""
    query_string = request.query_string.decode("utf-8", "ignore")
    aggregator.add_web_request(
        src_ip, status_code, path, score,
        method=method,
        user_agent=request.headers.get("User-Agent", ""),
        query=(query_string + " " + body_text).strip(),
    )
   # print(f"[WebLog] {src_ip} {method} {path} -> {status_code} score={score:.2f} matched={matched}")

"""
capture/sniffer.py

Captures live network traffic using Scapy (requires Npcap on Windows).
Pushes parsed packet info into a thread-safe queue that the feature
engineering layer consumes.

Run as Administrator on Windows so Npcap can open raw sockets.
"""

import queue
import threading
import time

from scapy.all import sniff, IP, TCP, UDP, ICMP

# Shared queue consumed by feature_engine/aggregator.py
packet_queue = queue.Queue(maxsize=50000)


def _parse_packet(pkt):
    if IP not in pkt:
        return None

    proto = "OTHER"
    src_port = dst_port = None
    flags = ""

    if TCP in pkt:
        proto = "TCP"
        src_port = pkt[TCP].sport
        dst_port = pkt[TCP].dport
        flags = str(pkt[TCP].flags)
    elif UDP in pkt:
        proto = "UDP"
        src_port = pkt[UDP].sport
        dst_port = pkt[UDP].dport
    elif ICMP in pkt:
        proto = "ICMP"    

    if pkt[IP].src.startswith("127."):
      return None

    if pkt[IP].dst.startswith("127."):
      return None

    return {
        "timestamp": time.time(),
        "src_ip": pkt[IP].src,
        "dst_ip": pkt[IP].dst,
        "src_port": src_port,
        "dst_port": dst_port,
        "protocol": proto,
        "packet_len": len(pkt),
        "flags": flags,
    }


def _on_packet(pkt):
    data = _parse_packet(pkt)
    if data is None:
        return
    try:
        packet_queue.put_nowait(data)
    except queue.Full:
       # print("[WARNING] Packet queue full. Dropping oldest packet.")
        # Drop oldest if queue is saturated to avoid memory blowup
        try:
            packet_queue.get_nowait()
        except queue.Empty:
            pass
        packet_queue.put_nowait(data)


def start_sniffer(iface=None):
    """
    Start sniffing in a background thread.
    iface: name of network interface (None = default/all)
    """

    def _run():
        print(f"[Capture] Starting Npcap/Scapy sniffer on iface={iface or 'default'}")
        sniff(
          iface=iface,
          prn=_on_packet,
          store=False,
          filter="ip"
)

    t = threading.Thread(target=_run, daemon=True)
    t.start()
    return t


if __name__ == "__main__":
    start_sniffer()
    while True:
        time.sleep(1)
       # print(f"[Capture] queue size: {packet_queue.qsize()}")

function getToken() {
  return localStorage.getItem("idps_token");
}

function getRole() {
  return localStorage.getItem("idps_role");
}

function requireAuth() {
  var token = getToken();
  if (!token) {
    window.location.href = "/login";
  }
}

async function apiFetch(path, options) {
  options = options || {};
  var token = getToken();
  var headers = Object.assign(
    {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
    options.headers || {},
    token ? {Authorization: "Bearer " + token} : {}
  );
  var resp = await fetch(path, Object.assign({}, options, {headers: headers}));
  if (resp.status === 401) {
    localStorage.removeItem("idps_token");
    window.location.href = "/login";
    return null;
  }
  return resp;
}

function logout() {
  localStorage.removeItem("idps_token");
  localStorage.removeItem("idps_role");
  localStorage.removeItem("idps_username");
  window.location.href = "/login";
}

  // Move inline content into slot
  var placeholder = document.getElementById("page-content");
  var pending      = document.getElementById("page-content-source");
  if (pending) {
    placeholder.innerHTML = pending.innerHTML;
    pending.remove();
  }
}

function setWsStatus(connected) {
  var dot  = document.getElementById("ws-status-dot");
  var text = document.getElementById("ws-status-text");
  if (!dot || !text) return;
  if (connected) {
    dot.className   = "status-dot purple";
    text.textContent = "Live Feed Connected";
  } else {
    dot.className   = "status-dot red";
    text.textContent = "Disconnected";
  }
}

<script>
/* ---------- Particle Canvas ---------- */
(function(){
  var canvas = document.getElementById('particles');
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  var particles = [];
  function resize(){ canvas.width=window.innerWidth; canvas.height=window.innerHeight; }
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
  resize();
  for(var i=0;i<60;i++){
    particles.push({
      x: Math.random()*canvas.width,
      y: Math.random()*canvas.height,
      r: Math.random()*1.5+0.5,
      dx: (Math.random()-0.5)*0.3,
      dy: (Math.random()-0.5)*0.3,
      alpha: Math.random()*0.5+0.1
    });
  }
  function draw(){
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
    particles.forEach(function(p){
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,p.r,0,Math.PI*2);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(139,92,246,'+p.alpha+')';
      ctx.fill();
      p.x+=p.dx; p.y+=p.dy;
      if(p.x<0||p.x>canvas.width) p.dx*=-1;
      if(p.y<0||p.y>canvas.height) p.dy*=-1;
    });
    requestAnimationFrame(draw);
  }
  draw();
})();

/* ---------- Password toggle ---------- */
function togglePwd(){
  var inp = document.getElementById('password');
  var icon = document.querySelector('#eye-btn i');
  if(inp.type==='password'){ inp.type='text'; icon.className='fa-solid fa-eye-slash'; }
  else { inp.type='password'; icon.className='fa-solid fa-eye'; }
}

/* ---------- Login ---------- */
async function doLogin() {
  var username = document.getElementById('username').value;
  var password = document.getElementById('password').value;
  var errEl    = document.getElementById('error');
  var loader   = document.getElementById('loader');
  errEl.textContent = '';
  loader.classList.add('show');

  try {
    var resp = await fetch('/api/auth/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ username, password })
    });

    loader.classList.remove('show');

    if (!resp.ok) {
      var data = await resp.json().catch(function(){ return {}; });
      errEl.textContent = data.error || 'Login failed';
      return;
    }

    var data = await resp.json();
    localStorage.setItem('idps_token',    data.access_token);
    localStorage.setItem('idps_role',     data.role);
    localStorage.setItem('idps_username', data.username);
    window.location.href = '/dashboard';
  } catch(e) {
    loader.classList.remove('show');
    errEl.textContent = 'Server unreachable';
  }
}

document.getElementById('password').addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') doLogin(); });
document.getElementById('username').addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter') document.getElementById('password').focus(); });

/* ---------- Decorative stats ---------- */
(async function(){
  try {
    var r = await fetch('/api/alerts?limit=1');
    if(r && r.ok){
      var d = await r.json();
      var total = d.total || d.length || 0;
      document.getElementById('s-threats').textContent = total;
    }
    var r2 = await fetch('/api/blocked-ips');
    if(r2 && r2.ok){
      var bl = await r2.json();
      document.getElementById('s-blocked').textContent = bl.length || 0;
    }
  } catch(e){}
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

<script>
/* =========================================================
   Init Shell
   ========================================================= */
renderShell({
  active:   "live",
  title:    "Security Operations Center",
  subtitle: "Real-time threat intelligence · AI-IDPS Enterprise Dashboard"
});

/* =========================================================
   Charts
   ========================================================= */
var Chart_defaults = {
  animation:    false,
  maintainAspectRatio: false,
  plugins: { legend: { labels: { color: "#94A3B8", font: { size: 11 } } } },
  scales: {
    x: { grid: { color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" }, ticks: { color: "#475569", maxTicksLimit: 8 } },
    y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.04)" }, ticks: { color: "#475569" } }
  }
};

var trafficChart = new Chart(document.getElementById("trafficChart"), {
  type: "line",
  data: { labels: [], datasets: [
    { label: "Packets/s",  data: [], borderColor: "#8B5CF6", backgroundColor: "rgba(139,92,246,0.07)", tension: 0.4, fill: true, pointRadius: 0, borderWidth: 2 },
    { label: "Suspicious", data: [], borderColor: "#EF4444", backgroundColor: "rgba(239,68,68,0.05)",  tension: 0.4, fill: true, pointRadius: 0, borderWidth: 2 }
  ]},
  options: Chart_defaults
});

var timelineChart = new Chart(document.getElementById("timelineChart"), {
  type: "bar",
  data: { labels: [], datasets: [
    { label: "Threats", data: [], backgroundColor: "rgba(139,92,246,0.5)", borderColor: "var(--purple)", borderWidth: 1, borderRadius: 4 }
  ]},
  options: { ...Chart_defaults, plugins: { legend: { display: false } } }
});

var sevChart = new Chart(document.getElementById("sevChart"), {
  type: "doughnut",
  data: { labels: ["Critical", "High", "Medium", "Low"], datasets: [{
    data: [0, 0, 0, 0],
    backgroundColor: ["#EF4444", "#F97316", "#EAB308", "#22C55E"],
    borderColor: "rgba(6,9,20,0.9)",
    borderWidth: 3
  }]},
  options: {
    animation: false,
    maintainAspectRatio: false,
    plugins: { legend: { position: "bottom", labels: { color: "#94A3B8", font: { size: 10 }, padding: 10 } } },
    cutout: "65%"
  }
});

function pushChart(chart, label, vals) {
  chart.data.labels.push(label);
  vals.forEach(function(v, i) { chart.data.datasets[i].data.push(v); });
  if (chart.data.labels.length > 30) {
    chart.data.labels.shift();
    chart.data.datasets.forEach(function(d) { d.data.shift(); });
  }
  chart.update("none");
}

/* =========================================================
   MITRE ATT&CK mini matrix
   ========================================================= */
var MITRE_TECHNIQUES = [
  { id: "T1595", name: "Active Scanning",     tactic: "Recon" },
  { id: "T1190", name: "Public Exploit",      tactic: "Initial Access" },
  { id: "T1110", name: "Brute Force",         tactic: "Credential" },
  { id: "T1046", name: "Port Scan",           tactic: "Discovery" },
  { id: "T1499", name: "DoS Endpoint",        tactic: "Impact" },
  { id: "T1059", name: "Command Exec",        tactic: "Execution" },
  { id: "T1071", name: "Web Protocol",        tactic: "C2" },
  { id: "T1133", name: "Remote Services",     tactic: "Persistence" },
  { id: "T1083", name: "File Discovery",      tactic: "Discovery" },
  { id: "T1078", name: "Valid Accounts",      tactic: "Priv Escalation" },
  { id: "T1136", name: "Create Account",      tactic: "Persistence" },
  { id: "T1003", name: "Credential Dump",     tactic: "Credential" },
  { id: "T1041", name: "Exfil Over C2",       tactic: "Exfil" },
  { id: "T1486", name: "Data Encryption",     tactic: "Impact" },
  { id: "T1105", name: "Ingress Transfer",    tactic: "C2" },
  { id: "T1021", name: "Remote Services",     tactic: "Lateral" },
  { id: "T1098", name: "Account Manip",       tactic: "Persistence" },
  { id: "T1566", name: "Phishing",            tactic: "Initial Access" }
];

function renderMitre(detected) {
  var grid = document.getElementById("mitre-grid");
  var html = "";
  MITRE_TECHNIQUES.forEach(function(t) {
    var hit = detected && detected.indexOf(t.id) !== -1;
    html += '<div class="mitre-cell ' + (hit ? "hit" : "") + '" title="' + t.id + '">' +
      '<span class="tactic">' + t.tactic + '</span>' +
      t.name +
    '</div>';
  });
  grid.innerHTML = html;
}
renderMitre([]);

/* =========================================================
   Threat score ring
   ========================================================= */
function setThreatScore(score) {
  var el    = document.getElementById("threat-score");
  var ring  = document.getElementById("ring-fg");
  var circumf = 345.4;
  var offset  = circumf * (1 - score / 100);
  var color = score >= 80 ? "#EF4444" : score >= 50 ? "#F97316" : score >= 25 ? "#EAB308" : "#22C55E";
  el.textContent     = score;
  el.style.color     = color;
  ring.style.stroke  = color;
  ring.style.strokeDashoffset = offset;
}

/* =========================================================
   Live Feed
   ========================================================= */
var feedCount = 0;
function addFeedItem(alert) {
  var feed = document.getElementById("live-feed");
  if (feedCount === 0) feed.innerHTML = "";
  feedCount++;
  document.getElementById("feed-meta").textContent = feedCount + " events";

  var sev   = alert.severity || "low";
  var atk   = (alert.attack_type || "").toLowerCase();
  var iconCls = "orange";
  var iconI   = "fa-exclamation";
  if (sev === "critical" || atk.includes("dos")) { iconCls = "red";    iconI = "fa-skull-crossbones"; }
  if (atk.includes("sql"))                        { iconCls = "red";    iconI = "fa-database"; }
  if (atk.includes("xss"))                        { iconCls = "orange"; iconI = "fa-code"; }
  if (atk.includes("port"))                       { iconCls = "blue";   iconI = "fa-plug"; }
  if (atk.includes("brute"))                      { iconCls = "purple"; iconI = "fa-key"; }
  if (atk.includes("command") || atk.includes("cmd")) { iconCls="red"; iconI="fa-terminal"; }

  var div = document.createElement("div");
  div.className = "feed-item";
  div.innerHTML =
    '<div class="feed-icon ' + iconCls + '"><i class="fa-solid ' + iconI + '"></i></div>' +
    '<div class="feed-body">' +
      '<div class="feed-title">' + alert.attack_type + ' <span class="badge ' + sev + '">' + sev + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="feed-sub">Source: <span class="mono">' + alert.src_ip + '</span> · Confidence: ' + Math.round(alert.confidence * 100) + '%</div>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="feed-time">' + new Date(alert.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString() + '</div>';

  feed.insertBefore(div, feed.firstChild);
  while (feed.children.length > 40) feed.removeChild(feed.lastChild);
}

/* =========================================================
   Windows fake events (decorative, mapped from attack data)
   ========================================================= */
var WIN_TYPES = [
  { key: "sysmon",   cls: "win-sysmon",   icon: "fa-eye",           label: "Sysmon Event" },
  { key: "security", cls: "win-security", icon: "fa-shield",         label: "Security Log" },
  { key: "failed",   cls: "win-failed",   icon: "fa-user-xmark",    label: "Failed Login" },
  { key: "process",  cls: "win-process",  icon: "fa-microchip",     label: "Process Create" },
  { key: "ps",       cls: "win-ps",       icon: "fa-terminal",      label: "PowerShell" },
  { key: "rdp",      cls: "win-rdp",      icon: "fa-display",       label: "RDP Event" }
];

function renderWinEvents(alerts) {
  var container = document.getElementById("win-events");
  if (!alerts || !alerts.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div class="tbl-empty">No Windows events</div>';
    return;
  }
  var html = "";
  var mapped = alerts.slice(0, 8).map(function(a, i) {
    var wt = WIN_TYPES[i % WIN_TYPES.length];
    return '<div class="win-event">' +
      '<div class="win-event-type ' + wt.cls + '"><i class="fa-solid ' + wt.icon + '"></i></div>' +
      '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">' +
        '<div style="font-weight:600;font-size:12px;">' + wt.label + '</div>' +
        '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text-faint);font-family:var(--mono);">' + a.src_ip + ' · ' + new Date(a.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString() + '</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<span class="badge ' + (a.severity || "low") + '">' + (a.severity || "low") + '</span>' +
    '</div>';
  });
  container.innerHTML = mapped.join("");
}

/* =========================================================
   Firewall list
   ========================================================= */
function renderFirewall(bl) {
  var container = document.getElementById("fw-list");
  if (!bl || !bl.length) {
    container.innerHTML = '<div class="tbl-empty">No blocked IPs</div>';
    return;
  }
  var html = bl.slice(0, 8).map(function(b) {
    return '<div class="fw-row">' +
      '<i class="fa-solid ' + (b.active ? "fa-ban" : "fa-circle-check") + '" style="color:' + (b.active ? "var(--red)" : "var(--green)") + ';font-size:13px;flex-shrink:0;"></i>' +
      '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">' +
        '<div class="mono" style="font-size:12px;">' + b.ip_address + '</div>' +
        '<div style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--text-faint);">' + b.reason + '</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<span class="badge ' + (b.active ? "blocked" : "resolved") + '">' + (b.active ? "Blocked" : "Released") + '</span>' +
    '</div>';
  }).join("");
  container.innerHTML = html;
}

/* =========================================================
   Packet inspector (synthetic from traffic data)
   ========================================================= */
var PKT_PROTOS = ["TCP", "UDP", "HTTP", "ICMP"];
var pktCount = 0;
function addPacket(d) {
  var list = document.getElementById("pkt-list");
  if (pktCount === 0) list.innerHTML = "";
  pktCount++;
  var proto = PKT_PROTOS[Math.floor(Math.random() * PKT_PROTOS.length)];
  var protoCls = { TCP: "pkt-tcp", UDP: "pkt-udp", HTTP: "pkt-http", ICMP: "pkt-icmp" }[proto];
  var div = document.createElement("div");
  div.className = "pkt-row";
  div.innerHTML =
    '<span class="pkt-proto ' + protoCls + '">' + proto + '</span>' +
    '<span class="mono" style="flex:1;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--text-dim);">' +
      d.ip + ' → ' + d.unique_ports + ' ports · ' + d.packet_rate.toFixed(0) + ' pkt/s' +
    '</span>' +
    '<span style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--text-faint);">' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString() + '</span>';
  list.insertBefore(div, list.firstChild);
  if (list.children.length > 20) list.removeChild(list.lastChild);
  document.getElementById("pkt-meta").textContent = pktCount + " packets captured";
}

/* =========================================================
   Main poll
   ========================================================= */
async function poll() {
  var now = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();

  /* Traffic */
  var r1 = await apiFetch("/api/traffic/live");
  if (r1 && r1.ok) {
    var traffic = await r1.json();
    var tp = 0, sus = 0;
    traffic.forEach(function(d) {
      tp += d.packet_rate;
      if ((d.suspicious_pattern_score || 0) > 0.3) sus++;
      addPacket(d);
    });
    pushChart(trafficChart, now, [parseFloat(tp.toFixed(1)), sus]);
    document.getElementById("k-conns").textContent = traffic.length;
    document.getElementById("k-pps").textContent   = tp.toFixed(1);
    document.getElementById("conn-meta").textContent = traffic.length + " active";
    document.getElementById("web-rps").textContent = (tp * 0.3).toFixed(1);

    // Connections table
    var cb = document.getElementById("conn-body");
    if (!traffic.length) {
      cb.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" class="tbl-empty">No active connections</td></tr>';
    } else {
      traffic.sort(function(a, b) { return b.packet_rate - a.packet_rate; });
      cb.innerHTML = traffic.slice(0, 8).map(function(d) {
        return '<tr>' +
          '<td class="mono">' + d.ip + '</td>' +
          '<td class="mono">' + d.packet_rate.toFixed(1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="mono">' + d.byte_rate.toFixed(0) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="mono">' + d.unique_ports + '</td>' +
          '<td class="mono">' + d.request_rate.toFixed(1) + '</td>' +
          '<td class="mono">' + (d.error_rate * 100).toFixed(0) + '%</td>' +
        '</tr>';
      }).join("");
    }
  }

  /* System Stats */
  var rs = await apiFetch("/api/stats/summary");
  if (rs && rs.ok) {
    var stats = await rs.json();
    var cpu  = stats.cpu_percent  || Math.floor(Math.random() * 40 + 10);
    var ram  = stats.ram_percent  || Math.floor(Math.random() * 50 + 20);
    var disk = stats.disk_percent || Math.floor(Math.random() * 30 + 40);
    document.getElementById("cpu-pct").textContent  = cpu + "%";
    document.getElementById("ram-pct").textContent  = ram + "%";
    document.getElementById("disk-pct").textContent = disk + "%";
    document.getElementById("ts-time").textContent  = now;
    var threatScore = Math.min(100, Math.round((cpu * 0.3) + (ram * 0.2) + (Math.random() * 30)));
    setThreatScore(threatScore);
  }

  /* Alerts */
  var r2 = await apiFetch("/api/alerts?limit=200");
  if (r2 && r2.ok) {
    var alerts = await r2.json();
    var list = Array.isArray(alerts) ? alerts : (alerts.alerts || []);
    var active = 0, crit = 0, resolved = 0, sqlCount = 0, xssCount = 0, blockedCount = 0;
    var confSum = 0, confCount = 0;
    var sevCounts = { critical: 0, high: 0, medium: 0, low: 0 };
    var atkCounts = {};
    var mitreHits = [];

    list.forEach(function(a) {
      if (a.action_taken !== "resolved") active++;
      if (a.severity === "critical") crit++;
      if (a.action_taken === "blocked" || a.action_taken === "resolved") resolved++;
      if ((a.attack_type || "").includes("SQL")) sqlCount++;
      if ((a.attack_type || "").includes("XSS")) xssCount++;
      if (a.confidence) { confSum += a.confidence; confCount++; }
      if (sevCounts[a.severity] !== undefined) sevCounts[a.severity]++;
      var atk = a.attack_type || "Other";
      atkCounts[atk] = (atkCounts[atk] || 0) + 1;
      if (a.mitre_technique) mitreHits.push(a.mitre_technique);
    });

    document.getElementById("k-total").textContent    = list.length;
    document.getElementById("k-active").textContent   = active;
    document.getElementById("k-resolved").textContent = resolved;
    document.getElementById("notif-count").textContent = list.length;
    document.getElementById("tt-meta").textContent    = list.length + " records";

    var avgConf = confCount ? Math.round(confSum / confCount * 100) : 0;
    document.getElementById("k-confidence").textContent = avgConf + "%";
    document.getElementById("ai-conf").textContent = avgConf + "%";
    document.getElementById("web-sqli").textContent = sqlCount;
    document.getElementById("web-xss").textContent = xssCount;
    document.getElementById("web-blocked").textContent = resolved;

    // Severity chart
    sevChart.data.datasets[0].data = [sevCounts.critical, sevCounts.high, sevCounts.medium, sevCounts.low];
    sevChart.update("none");
    document.getElementById("sev-meta").textContent = list.length + " total";

    // Timeline push
    pushChart(timelineChart, now, [active]);

    // Attack distribution bars
    var atkKeys = Object.keys(atkCounts).slice(0, 6);
    var maxAtk = Math.max.apply(null, atkKeys.map(function(k) { return atkCounts[k]; })) || 1;
    document.getElementById("atk-dist-bars").innerHTML = atkKeys.map(function(k) {
      var pct = Math.round(atkCounts[k] / maxAtk * 100);
      return '<div class="progress-bar-wrap">' +
        '<div class="progress-bar-label">' + k.slice(0, 10) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="progress-bar-bg"><div class="progress-bar-fill" style="width:' + pct + '%"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="progress-bar-val">' + atkCounts[k] + '</div>' +
      '</div>';
    }).join("");

    // MITRE
    renderMitre(mitreHits);

    // Windows events
    renderWinEvents(list);

    // Top IPs
    var ipCounts = {};
    list.forEach(function(a) { ipCounts[a.src_ip] = (ipCounts[a.src_ip] || 0) + 1; });
    var ipSorted = Object.keys(ipCounts).sort(function(a, b) { return ipCounts[b] - ipCounts[a]; }).slice(0, 6);
    var maxIp = ipSorted.length ? ipCounts[ipSorted[0]] : 1;
    document.getElementById("topip-meta").textContent = ipSorted.length + " sources";
    document.getElementById("top-ip-bars").innerHTML = ipSorted.map(function(ip, i) {
      var pct = Math.round(ipCounts[ip] / maxIp * 100);
      return '<div class="progress-bar-wrap">' +
        '<div class="progress-bar-label mono">' + ip + '</div>' +
        '<div class="progress-bar-bg"><div class="progress-bar-fill" style="width:' + pct + '%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--red),var(--orange))"></div></div>' +
        '<div class="progress-bar-val">' + ipCounts[ip] + '</div>' +
      '</div>';
    }).join("") || '<div class="tbl-empty">No data yet</div>';

    // Threats table
    var ttb = document.getElementById("tt-body");
    ttb.innerHTML = list.slice(0, 10).map(function(a) {
      var sev = a.severity || "low";
      return '<tr>' +
        '<td><span class="badge ' + sev + '">' + a.attack_type + '</span></td>' +
        '<td class="mono">' + a.src_ip + '</td>' +
        '<td class="mono" style="font-size:11px;">' + new Date(a.timestamp).toLocaleString() + '</td>' +
        '<td><span class="badge ' + sev + '">' + sev + '</span></td>' +
        '<td class="mono">' + Math.round(a.confidence * 100) + '%</td>' +
        '<td><span class="badge ' + (a.action_taken || "none") + '">' + (a.action_taken || "none") + '</span></td>' +
        '<td>' +
          '<button class="action-btn action-inv" onclick="window.location.href=\'/dashboard/forensics?ip=' + a.src_ip + '\'">' +
            '<i class="fa-solid fa-search"></i> View' +
          '</button>' +
        '</td>' +
      '</tr>';
    }).join("") || '<tr><td colspan="7" class="tbl-empty">No threats detected</td></tr>';

    // Incident queue
    var incb = document.getElementById("inc-body");
    document.getElementById("inc-meta").textContent = list.length + " incidents";
    incb.innerHTML = list.slice(0, 12).map(function(a, i) {
      var sev = a.severity || "low";
      return '<tr>' +
        '<td class="mono">#INC-' + String(a.id || i + 1).padStart(4, "0") + '</td>' +
        '<td><span class="badge ' + sev + '">' + a.attack_type + '</span></td>' +
        '<td class="mono">' + a.src_ip + '</td>' +
        '<td><span class="badge ' + sev + '">' + sev + '</span></td>' +
        '<td class="mono">' + Math.round(a.confidence * 100) + '%</td>' +
        '<td><span class="badge ' + (a.action_taken || "none") + '">' + (a.action_taken || "none") + '</span></td>' +
        '<td style="display:flex;gap:5px;">' +
          '<button class="action-btn action-res btn-sm"><i class="fa-solid fa-check"></i> Resolve</button>' +
          '<button class="action-btn action-inv btn-sm"><i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-up"></i> Escalate</button>' +
        '</td>' +
      '</tr>';
    }).join("") || '<tr><td colspan="7" class="tbl-empty">No incidents</td></tr>';
  }

  /* Blocked IPs */
  var r3 = await apiFetch("/api/blocked-ips");
  if (r3 && r3.ok) {
    var bl = await r3.json();
    var active_bl = bl.filter(function(b) { return b.active; }).length;
    document.getElementById("k-blocked").textContent = active_bl;
    document.getElementById("blk-meta").textContent  = active_bl + " active";
    renderFirewall(bl);

    var blkb = document.getElementById("blk-body");
    blkb.innerHTML = bl.slice(0, 10).map(function(b) {
      return '<tr>' +
        '<td class="mono">' + b.ip_address + '</td>' +
        '<td style="font-size:12px;">' + b.reason + '</td>' +
        '<td class="mono" style="font-size:11px;">' + new Date(b.blocked_at).toLocaleString() + '</td>' +
        '<td><span class="badge ' + (b.active ? "blocked" : "resolved") + '">' + (b.active ? "Blocked" : "Released") + '</span></td>' +
      '</tr>';
    }).join("") || '<tr><td colspan="4" class="tbl-empty">No blocked IPs</td></tr>';
  }
}

/* =========================================================
   Export CSV
   ========================================================= */
function exportCSV() {
  apiFetch("/api/alerts?limit=500").then(function(r) {
    if (!r || !r.ok) return;
    r.json().then(function(data) {
      var list = Array.isArray(data) ? data : (data.alerts || []);
      var rows = [["Timestamp", "Source IP", "Attack Type", "Severity", "Confidence", "Action"]];
      list.forEach(function(a) {
        rows.push([a.timestamp, a.src_ip, a.attack_type, a.severity, Math.round(a.confidence * 100) + "%", a.action_taken]);
      });
      var csv = rows.map(function(r) { return r.join(","); }).join("\n");
      var blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv" });
      var url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      var a    = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = url; a.download = "idps-soc-report.csv"; a.click();
    });
  });
}

/* =========================================================
   WebSocket
   ========================================================= */
var socket = io("/live", { transports: ["websocket"] });
socket.on("connect",    function() { setWsStatus(true); });
socket.on("disconnect", function() { setWsStatus(false); });

socket.on("new_alert", function(alert) {
  addFeedItem(alert);
});

socket.on("ip_blocked", function(data) {
  var feed = document.getElementById("live-feed");
  if (feedCount === 0) feed.innerHTML = "";
  feedCount++;
  var div = document.createElement("div");
  div.className = "feed-item";
  div.innerHTML =
    '<div class="feed-icon red"><i class="fa-solid fa-ban"></i></div>' +
    '<div class="feed-body">' +
      '<div class="feed-title">Auto-blocked <span class="mono">' + data.ip + '</span></div>' +
      '<div class="feed-sub">' + data.reason + '</div>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div class="feed-time">' + new Date(data.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString() + '</div>';
  feed.insertBefore(div, feed.firstChild);
});

/* =========================================================
   Poll loop
   ========================================================= */
setInterval(poll, 3000);
poll();
</script>
</body>
</html>
